<tr><td><a id="row_5_19_3_11_"></a><span style="width:64px;display:inline-block;"> </span><a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/bflb__bl60x__clock_8h_source.html">源码（Source）</a><a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/bflb__bl60x__clock_8h.html">bflb_bl60x_clock.h</a></td><td><span data-translation="610">BL60x的性能情况较为复杂：BCLK分频器（Divider）是影响BL60x性能最重要的调节因素，这似乎与向CPU供给数据的硬件设计问题有关。在其他所有BFLB片上系统（SoC）中，BCLK对纯CPU性能大体上并不重要，其中也包括采用相同缓存和CPU方案的BL70x、BL70xL。</span><br><small lang="en" data-source-prose="610">Performance on BL60x is a complex affair: BCLK divider is the most important scaler for performance on BL60x due to what appears as a hardware design issue relating to feeding the CPU, as BCLK is mostly unimportant for raw CPU performance on all other BFLB SoCs, including BL70x and BL70xL which use the same cache and CPU schemes </small></td></tr>

<tr><td><a id="row_5_19_3_12_"></a><span style="width:64px;display:inline-block;"> </span><a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/bflb__bl616cl__clock_8h_source.html">源码（Source）</a><a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/bflb__bl616cl__clock_8h.html">bflb_bl616cl_clock.h</a></td><td><span data-translation="611">要安全运行，必须设置BCLK分频器（Divider），使BCLK不超过80 MHz。不过，已有观察表明硬件能够容忍最高130 MHz的BCLK。</span><br><small lang="en" data-source-prose="611">BCLK divider MUST be set so BCLK is 80MHz or slower to operate safely, However BCLK of up to 130 MHz have been observed to be tolerated </small></td></tr>

<tr><td><a id="row_5_19_3_13_"></a><span style="width:64px;display:inline-block;"> </span><a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/bflb__bl61x__clock_8h_source.html">源码（Source）</a><a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/bflb__bl61x__clock_8h.html">bflb_bl61x_clock.h</a></td><td><span data-translation="612">要安全运行，必须设置BCLK分频器（Divider），使BCLK不超过80 MHz。不过，已有观察表明硬件能够容忍最高130 MHz的BCLK。</span><br><small lang="en" data-source-prose="612">BCLK divider MUST be set so BCLK is 80MHz or slower to operate safely, However BCLK of up to 130 MHz have been observed to be tolerated </small></td></tr>
